**FIRST ACTION (s274 SAVE 11:37 PM ET 10-28 REAL CLOCK, NEWEST, read first; the s273 11:31pm block below is the task spec and still stands).** Chris 11:36pm "need to update claude when you get a moment" = saved so he can restart the app. On resume: NO questions, no recap, one plain line, "Switch to Fable, slider High" if he is not on it, then CONTINUE the in-app web screen task (s273 11:31pm block, parts a/b/c) from where it stopped: DONE = read-through of src/lib/server/appSession.ts, auth.ts, sessions.ts, api/auth/login + me, proxy.ts, app/app/layout.tsx, AppHeader, BottomTabBar, Footer, MarketingNav, PublicBottomNav, next.config.ts; `npx expo install react-native-webview` done (14.0.1, in mobile/package.json, this commit). FINDINGS that shape the build: (1) react-native-webview has NO web implementation (lib/WebView.js renders "does not support this platform") = web.tsx renders an <iframe> on Platform.OS web, WebView on native; (2) X-Frame-Options SAMEORIGIN in next.config.ts blocks the 8081->3000 iframe = dev-only: drop that header + add Access-Control-Allow-Credentials true to the dev CORS block (never in production); localhost ports are the SAME SITE so Lax cookies flow in the iframe and a credentials:'include' fetch from 8081 stores the 3000 cookie; (3) the /app pages use AppHeader + BottomTabBar + the layout's <footer> (not MarketingNav; /pricing uses MarketingNav + Footer). DESIGN decided: app mode = inline <script> at the top of <body> in src/app/layout.tsx sets html[data-app="1"] when ?app=1 is in the query or cookie cardflip_app=1 (static pages stay static; add suppressHydrationWarning on <html>); globals.css `html[data-app="1"] [data-app-hide]{display:none!important}` + `[data-app-shell]{--bottom-nav-h:0px!important}`; put data-app-hide on AppHeader <header>, BottomTabBar <nav>, the /app layout <footer>, MarketingNav <header>, Footer <footer>, and the account page's "Add to Home Screen" push row (Safari words). Handoff = src/app/api/auth/app-cookie/route.ts: requires x-cardflip-app + Bearer (lib/server/appSession.ts bearerToken), getSessionUserId, needs a new sessions.ts export for the row's expires_at; sets cardflip_session (sessionCookieOptions) + cf_home (setHomeCookie) + cardflip_app=1 (not httpOnly, 1 year); GET ?next=/app/... (relative path only, no //) -> 303 to next+app=1; POST -> 204; add "/api/auth/app-cookie" to proxy.ts GATE_EXEMPT_PREFIXES. App: mobile/src/app/web.tsx (params path + title; native = WebView source {uri: API_URL+/api/auth/app-cookie?next=path, headers: authorization + x-cardflip-app}; web = fetch POST credentials include then iframe API_URL+path+?app=1; native top bar "‹ Back" + title; mailto/tel -> Linking; a navigation to /login, /signup or / closes the screen + auth refresh()); register "web" in root _layout Stack; Home rows Account /app/account, Packs /app/packs, Wishlist /app/wishlist, Rewards /app/rewards, Help /app/help, Price check /app/price-check, Subscribe /pricing. Mobile web preview: add launch.json config "cardflip-mobile-web" = npm run web --prefix mobile, port 8081. Tests: tsc + expo lint in mobile/, npm run lint at root, curl the handoff, pane check of /app/account?app=1 at phone width, screenshots to Chris. **FIRST ACTION (s246 SAVE 11:55 PM ET 10-24 REAL CLOCK (Get-Date), NEWEST, read first).** Chris 11:53pm: "save, clear and resume with going ahead with the one piece picture fix" = his YES to the One Piece wrong-picture fix (TASKS B7; before/after sheet docs/shots/onepiece-wrong-pictures-before-after.jpg). NO questions on resume, no recap. WHERE WE ARE: fix measured, dry-run, eyeballed; NOT applied, nothing written to the catalog yet. I WAS ABOUT TO port it into the real script and apply it. THE FIX, in order: (1) BACKUP image_url / own_image_url / own_image_src / own_tile_url of every One Piece row (local data/cardflip.db tcg_cards game='onepiece') + the same columns on prod (SELECT via .env.migration.json) for the 361 ids in data/op-picture-fix-dry-2026-10-24.jsonl -> data/op-picture-fix-backup-2026-10-25.json. (2) PORT the picker from the untracked dry-run copy scripts/_op-images-own-set.mjs into scripts/sync-onepiece-images.mjs (CRLF file; Bash eats backslashes = Edit/Write only for regex lines): groups carry g.name + market (tcgcsv /prices, max marketPrice per product); rows select set_name + price_usd; foldSet/setWords/sameSet ("ST-05: Starter Deck 5 Film Edition" = "Starter Deck 5: Film Edition"; "vol 2" must match both sides), WORDS (variant -> product-name words: special=sp/special, gold, silver, manga, alt-art/parallel, full-art, spr, tr=treasure, gold-stamped-signature=signature ...), ratio, promoGroup (promotion|pre-release|tournament|release event|anniversary|championship|judge|winner|participant). Pick = (a) the same-number product within 15% of the row's own price that is own set / says the printing word / same class (sort: own set, word, closest) > (b) own set + class or word, in pairing order, dropped when 2x+ off the row price > (c) the old pick (number + class across every set) when own set, or not a promo group and within 2x > (d) Bandai's ref picture. Best: the pick as a pure function (scripts/lib/onepiecePick.mjs) + scripts/test-onepiece-images.mjs in npm test with the measured cases: OP10-065_p1 Sugar SP -> 671455 (not Judge Pack 641237); OP13-118_p3 $7,500 -> 657401 Red SAA, OP13-118_p2 $2,116.43 -> 657402; OP17-062#3630 $172.30 -> 712091 SAA, OP17-062_p1 $12.95 -> 707135; OP09-004_p6 silver $1,070.71 -> 657443 (not gold 657442); ST01-012_p3 -> 529850; OP01-120_p2 $1,532.80 -> no product = Bandai. I asked him for 5 labeled rounds (loose card light off / same card in binder light off / light on / black-border card / tilt sweep) and NONE arrived; he went quiet ~3:30pm. RESULT, MEASURED (scripts/outline-eval/eval.mjs + labels.json, ten hand-labeled binder frames, IoU >= 0.9 = correct): the ORIGINAL finder = 2/10 correct, mean IoU 0.73; every variant tried today (narrow guide band, same-sign guard, Theil-Sen fit, aspect window, border uniformity, polarity, nested-box pocket rule, edge continuation, coarse box search, content/detail mask, guide-size prior) = 0-2/10, mean 0.64-0.73. So src/lib/photoQuality.ts is REVERTED to HEAD (unchanged). The line-based finder is at its limit on binder pages: pocket seams and sleeve highlights fit straight lines better (share 0.97-1.0) than the card's own edge seen through plastic (scattered +-4 px, share 0.5-0.7, wiped out by glare), and every line-level cue has a sleeve structure that mimics it. WHAT IS PHYSICALLY UNIQUE TO A CARD (verified on the full-res JPEGs with scripts/outline-eval/corner.mjs): ROUNDED CORNERS, r ~ 3 mm = ~50 px at 1920 (invisible at the 224-px working crop), crisp edge against the sleeve; seam corners are sharp stitched T-junctions, pocket tops are open, highlights have no corners. NEXT (new session, Fable max): a full-res rounded-corner DETECTOR - not sampling at predicted corners (tried: +-15 px prediction error and a ~10 px soft edge turned far/near/arc into noise) but a template search for a quarter-circle edge at 2-3 radii near each guide corner (+-15 % of the guide) on a half-res (960 px) frame, orientation fixed per corner, then the quad from the 4 corners (lines only as a check); budget ~40 ms/tick; MEASURE WITH THE HARNESS BEFORE THE PHONE. Second, independent of the finder: TWITCH fix in CameraCapture ~line 700 = hysteresis (accept a jump > 8 % of the size only after 3 agreeing ticks; keep the last outline ~500 ms through a miss). HARNESS: node --experimental-strip-types scripts/outline-eval/eval.mjs [stampFilter] (DETAIL=1 lines per side, PEAKS=left|right|top|bottom row peaks, COMBOS=1 every combination vs label - needs trace.combos re-added to the finder); grid.mjs renders a 3x crop with a 10-px grid for labeling; zoom.mjs a 10x region; corner.mjs a full-res 160-px patch round a crop point; sheet.cjs contact sheets. .outline-dumps is gitignored and local.
